--- Dependency 4 ---
id: art_8OlSrcw-hzgO
type: dataset
title: Who Edited This Model, and How
summary: |-
  Ships ONE schema-validated full_data_out.json with five datasets (7,381 examples, 16.5 MiB) in three blocks. DATA ONLY: no weights downloaded, no forward passes, no training, no W01-W05, no AUROC, $0.00 OpenRouter spend. Built offline by `uv run data.py` from temp/datasets/ + results/ (48 deps pinned exactly in pyproject.toml).

  BLOCK 1 `edit_manifest` (672 rows = 513 edited + 159 parents). Harvested from 61 Hub sweeps (20 search terms, 20 uploaders, 20 architectures, 1 global top-downloads) over 20,313 enumerated repos. Spans **189 distinct uploaders** against the plan's floor of 5 -- iteration 2's 8 positives came from only TWO uploaders, so this directly removes that confound. **6 of 7** recipe classes populated: R1_GLOBAL_RANK1_DIM 78, R2_NORM_PRESERVING_PROJECTED 20, R3_MULTIDIRECTION_SVD 26, R4_PARTIAL_LAYER_OR_PER_HEAD 235, R6_BEHAVIOURAL_SFT_UNCENSORED 19, R7_MERGE_OF_ABLITERATED 15, UNKNOWN 120. **388 complete parent-child pairs** for the H3 head-to-head; all 8 iteration-2 members present and flagged `is_iter2_class_member`; 1,536 over-ceiling near-misses recorded separately; every row `status=ok`.

  THREE NUMBERS THAT SHOULD DRIVE THE PAPER. (i) **UNKNOWN = 23.4%** of edited rows: nearly a quarter of self-declared edited checkpoints name no mechanism, which is the ceiling on Hub recipe provenance. (ii) **repo_id_contains_abliteration_string = 50.5%** (259/513): a plain regex on the repo id alone already solves HALF the detection task, so that -- not chance -- is the baseline any detector must beat. This is the reviewer's previously unmeasured point, now quantified. (iii) **R5_SPECTRAL_CASCADE_DCT is EMPTY**, and that is a finding, not a gap: the OBLITERATUS README we fetched contains ZERO occurrences of 'spectral', 'frequency', 'Fourier' or 'DCT' (its profiles are basic/advanced/aggressive/surgical/optimized/inverted over diff-in-means, SVD, whitened SVD). Any H1 arm needing a frequency-domain recipe is UNRUNNABLE at this scale.

  BLOCK 2, three laundering corpora. 2a `sft_benign` 3,370 English single-turn pairs from OpenAssistant/oasst1 (Apache-2.0, sha fdf72ae0), 627 safety-topic pairs and 6,695 duplicate instructions dropped. 2b `fluency_wikitext` 1,000 paragraphs from Salesforce/wikitext wikitext-2-raw-v1 test (sha b08601e0), median 148 GPT-2 tokens, 163,496 total; the @-@ artifact is documented, not silently carried. 2c `heldout_benign_prompts` 200 prompts from databricks-dolly-15k (sha bdd27f4d) -- a DIFFERENT repo from 2a, then exact dedupe (1 dropped) and 5-gram Jaccard >= 0.5 (0 dropped); measured max Jaccard vs any 2a instruction is **0.273**. NC sources excluded throughout (alpaca, no_robots rejected).

  BLOCK 3 `hub_scan_pool` 2,139 metadata-only rows, all strata floors beaten: 407 declared / 1,105 non-declaring chat / 627 non-declaring base. Ranked by `scan_rank` (undeclared chat by descending downloads first) with `cumulative_bytes`, so a scan stopping at rank k has a stateable coverage and a cost in GB; 7.3 TB total with per-decile cumulative gigabytes.

  INTEGRITY, ALL VERIFIED ON THE SHIPPED FILE: 0 rows with a missing or 'main' sha; 0 rows missing a param count; 0 rows above either ceiling; **482/482 recipe_evidence spans verified as verbatim substrings of the cards they cite (0 fabricated)**; 482/482 carry an evidence_url; 0 parent rows wrongly carrying a recipe_class; 2a leaks no safety terms and has no duplicates.

  TWO BUG CLASSES FOUND AND FIXED, both consequential downstream. (1) A three-seed 10-row hand-check (27/30 survived, failures and objections recorded in coverage.block_1.hand_check) exposed four labeller defects, including 'trained' matching inside `from_pretrained(...)` in a usage snippet and corpus-sense 'unfiltered' labelling a pedagogy study as an uncensoring fine-tune. (2) **The Hub's safetensors index is not always right**: samuelcardillo/Qwen3-Coder-Next-Opus-4.6-Reasoning-Distilled reports 6,208,256 parameters while shipping 159 GB of shards, and two 35B checkpoints report 664,944. Taking it at face value silently admits 32-35B models into a sub-4B pool. The ceiling is now enforced TWICE -- once from the index, once from on-disk safetensors bytes divided by the repo's widest declared dtype -- which rejected 25 such rows. Any downstream artifact resolving parameter counts from the Hub should apply the same cross-check.

  A bare 'this is an abliterated version' is deliberately labelled UNKNOWN/ambiguous rather than folded into R1, which would have inflated R1 until the class meant nothing. Ten HF dataset candidates were downloaded and evaluated; three are shipped and each of the seven other verdicts is recorded in metadata.dataset_selection (GAIR/lima is gated; tulu-3-sft-mixture is partly non-commercial; oasst2/oasst_top1/guanaco are not independent of 2a and guanaco is multilingual).

{
  "title": "Does dequantizing bring the scar back?",
  "summary": "Two-arm experiment on the archived abliteration 'laundering ladder'. ARM 1 splits the quantization finding and runs the never-run remedy: take the int8/nf4/int4 round-trips of the archived Qwen3-1.7B abliteration root, dequantize back to fp16, recompute the weight statistics W01-W05, and — because plain dequantization only recovers the rounded values, not the original ones — additionally test a noise-floor-relative statistic (W05rel = min-layer energy divided by the random-direction energy floor) that is the actual candidate remedy, plus a rounding-noise sweep and a per-layer energy profile showing WHICH layers lost the suppression. ARM 2 puts Wilson intervals on every harmful-refusal rate in the ladder, re-scores the three load-bearing stages per family at n>=100 harmful items instead of 40, and adds TWO new roots — an in-house depth-weighted (mlabonne-style Gaussian kernel) abliteration of Qwen3-1.7B and a uniform all-layer abliteration of Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct — each run through the three cheapest laundering families (linear merge with parent, quantization round-trip, add-back-all) so the crossing intensities become a range rather than a single-root anecdote. Direction selection is BEHAVIOURAL (dev-10 refusal sweep, AUROC only as tie-break and sensitivity row), because held-out AUROC saturates. Ships one machine-readable crossing table, per-root figures with intervals on both axes, and a full reproduction-gate delta report against the archived iteration-3 values.",

A point mismatch INSIDE that interval is a PASS -- the\n#       whole point of this experiment is that 40 items do not resolve 0.03.\n#   (f) Recompute the 5 statistics for 3 randomly chosen archived ladder stages and\n#       report max |delta|. This is the ladder-level gate.\n#   If (c) fails: STOP the rebuild path and fall back to re-abliterating from scratch\n#   using root.json's l_star=18 + the layer_contrast dataset (see fallback plan).\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# S1. SHARED HARNESS                                    (target: 30 min)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# S1.1 Behavioural scoring (lib_behave.py) -- reuse the archived rubric-B judge VERBATIM.\n#   Extract the exact rubric-B prompt string from A3/gen_art_experiment_2/lib_*.py\n#   (grep for 'rubric' / 'judge'). Log every call to results/cost.jsonl with a running total;\n#     ABORT the arm and write partial results if cumulative >= $1.35 (0.9 * 1.50 cap).\n#   Wilson 95% interval on every rate, always, no exceptions.\n#\n# S1.2 Fluency: wikitext_ppl over the 200 wikitext_fluency passages (same passages as\n#   the archive; verify the archived parent value 27.077 reproduces to <1%), and\n#   distinct-3 on the generations. Fluency screen = distinct3 >= 0.5 * parent distinct3.\n#   Report perplexity BESIDE refusal but never as the screen (the screen is distinct-3).\n#\n# S1.3 Weight statistics (lib_wstats.py):\n#   w_stats(state_dict, n_layers, hidden) -> dict with\n#     W01 suppression depth, W02 direction consistency, W03 gap vs random (256 dirs --\n#       keep 256, the draft said 64), W04 isolation, W05 = log10 min_l e_l(v1),\n#       W05q10 = log10 p10_l e_l(v1),\n#     e_v1 : per-write-matrix energy vector (length = 2 * n_layers)  <-- the per-layer\n#       energy PROFILE the direction asks for; always emit it.\n#   NEW STATISTIC (this experiment's contribution to ARM 1):\n#     W05rel = log10( min_l e_l(v1) / median_{d,l} e_l(u_d) )\n#       where u_d are the same 256 random unit directions used by W03.\n#       Rationale, stated as a falsifiable prediction: 4-bit rounding raises the energy\n#       floor in EVERY direction, so an ABSOLUTE minimum (W05) can be lifted above the\n#       threshold while the null direction is still relatively empty. If W05rel separates\n#       quantized-abliterated from quantized-clean where W05 does not, the limitation\n#       shrinks to 'score the ratio, not the absolute'. If it does not, say so plainly:\n#       4-bit rounding genuinely destroys the null direction.\n#   Threshold used throughout: TAU = -2.7415117804288127 (panel-fitted; state in output\n#     that it was never validated out of panel).\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# S2. ARM 1 -- THE DEQUANTIZATION REMEDY                (target: 60 min)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# CRITICAL FRAMING THE EXECUTOR MUST RESOLVE FIRST, BEFORE RUNNING ANYTHING:\n#   Read the archived int4 ladder row in ladder.jsonl and its producing code. Say this explicitly in the output and\n#     run the SUBSTANTIVE version below instead. Either way ARM 1 produces a result.\n#\n# S2.1 Build the quantization ladder from the rebuilt root (and, in S5, from each new root):\n#   for scheme in ['int8', 'nf4', 'fp4', 'int4_gptq_free']:\n#       bnb round-trip: load with BitsAndBytesConfig(load_in_8bit / load_in_4bit,\n#         bnb_4bit_quant_type=scheme, bnb_4bit_compute_dtype=bf16,\n#         bnb_4bit_use_double_quant=True), then DEQUANTIZE every Linear back to fp16\n#         via bitsandbytes.functional.dequantize_4bit(w.data, w.quant_state)  (8-bit:\n#         int8_vectorwise_dequant / reconstruct from CB * SCB / 127).\n#       Also implement a dependency-free reference: symmetric per-output-channel\n#         round-to-nearest at 8/4/3 bits, so the sweep does not die if bitsandbytes\n#         misbehaves on this GPU. Report BOTH bnb and reference for int4 as a\n#         cross-check; if they disagree by >0.1 log10 on W05, report the discrepancy.\n#   ROUNDING-NOISE SWEEP (the 'how much rounding does it take' question):\n#       bits in [8, 6, 5, 4, 3] using the reference quantizer -> W01..W05, W05rel, e_v1.\n#       Emit the full curve; the crossing bit-width where W05 rises above TAU is the\n#       reportable number.\n#\n# S2.2 For every quantized+dequantized checkpoint compute:\n#   - W01..W05, W05q10, W05rel\n#   - the per-write-matrix energy profile e_v1 (length 56) and, for the paper, the\n#     LAYER INDICES whose energy rose most -- 'which layers lost the suppression'\n#   - cos(v1_quantized, r_original): does quantization rotate the recovered eigenvector\n#     away from the injected direction? (If cos collapses, the minimum is being taken\n#     over the wrong direction -- a mechanistically different failure from 'the null\n#     filled in', and worth separating.)\n#   - behaviour: harmful refusal (n>=100, S3 pool), xstest over-refusal (n=50),\n#     wikitext_ppl, distinct-3. EXPECT ppl 26.25 -> ~37.7 (+43%) at int4; report the\n#     measured value and state that 'evades for free' overstates it.\n#\n# S2.3 VERDICT, one of three, written into method_out.json as a literal string:\n#   SCAR_RETURNS          -- W05 (or W05rel) back below TAU after dequantization.\n#                            Then Method must state dequantization as a PREPROCESSING\n#                            REQUIREMENT, not a limitation.\n#   SCAR_STAYS_GONE       -- ship the per-layer profile + the bit-width sweep showing\n#                            how much rounding noise is needed.\n#   SCAR_PARTIALLY_RETURNS-- ship the profile and the recovered margin (W05 - TAU).\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# S3. Per family, the three\n#   cells that matter are: the root, the first intensity where the FLAG dies\n#   (W05 crosses TAU upward), and the first intensity where UN-CENSORING dies (refusal\n#   returns to within one Wilson half-width of the parent's 0.923).\n#   Item pool: from D_PROMPTS plain_harmful, take the 80 rows with meta.in_core80 == True\n#   plus 40 more sampled stratified by the 10 categories with a FIXED seed\n#   (seed = 20260813, recorded) -> n = 120, a superset of the archived core40 so the\n#   40-item value is recomputable from the same run for direct comparability.\n#   Score each such cell at n = 120 (achieved n reported; some completions may be empty).\n#   SE at p=0.2 drops from ~0.075 to ~0.037. ARM 2c -- THREE LAUNDERING FAMILIES x EACH ROOT    (target: 90 min)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nROOTS = {A: archived Qwen3-1.7B uniform (rebuilt),\n         B: Qwen3-1.7B depth-weighted Gaussian,\n         C: Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct uniform}\nFAMILIES = {\n  'merge'   : w in [0.10, 0.25, 0.50, 0.75]   # W = (1-w)*W_root + w*W_parent\n  'quant'   : ['int8', 'nf4', 'int4']         # round-trip then dequantize to fp16\n  'addback' : eps in [0.05, 0.10, 0.25, 0.50, 1.00]\n                                              # W += eps * outer(r, r @ W_parent)\n                                              # ('add-back-ALL': every edited matrix)\n}\nfor root in ROOTS:                    # root A included so families are comparable\n  for family, intensities in FAMILIES:\n    for x in intensities:\n      sd = apply(family, x, root)\n      wst = w_stats(sd)                            # W01..W05, W05q10, W05rel, e_v1\n      beh = score_stage(sd, harmful_120 if load_bearing else harmful_40,\n                        xstest_50, wikitext_200)\n      emit row -> results/crossing_table.jsonl\n# Load-bearing detection is two-pass: pass 1 at n=40 over the whole grid to LOCATE the\n# crossings cheaply; pass 2 re-scores only the located cells (+ their neighbours) at\n# n=120. This keeps generation cost bounded while spending the extra items exactly where\n# the decimals matter.\n# Total generation: ~3 roots x 12 intensities x (40 or 120 harmful + 50 xstest) x 64 tok\n# on <=1.7B models -> well inside the GPU budget at batch 16.\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# S6. ANALYSIS                                          (target: 45 min)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# S6.1 Per (root, family): find\n#   flag_death_intensity  = smallest x with W05 > TAU   (linear interpolation reported\n#                           alongside the discrete grid point; the GRID point is primary)\n#   uncensor_death_intensity = smallest x with harmful refusal Wilson-lower-bound above\n#                           the root's Wilson-upper-bound\n#   ORDER = which dies first. THE ORDERING IS THE RESULT; the decimals are not.\n# S6.2 Test the pre-stated prediction: does root B (non-uniform) start closer to TAU and\n#   die sooner than roots A and C? Report the margin (W05 - TAU) at intensity 0 for all\n#   three roots and the flag_death_intensity for each family. Verdict string, one of:\n#   NONUNIFORM_DIES_SOONER / NO_ROOT_EFFECT / LADDER_UNDEFINED_FOR_NONUNIFORM_ROOT.\n# S6.3 Cross-architecture: does root C (Llama) give the same crossing intensities as\n#   root A (Qwen)? Report both; a range replaces the single-root point estimate\n#   ('the flag dies at w in [x_lo, x_hi] across 2 uniform roots on 2 architectures').\n# S6.4 Threshold brittleness, free re-analysis: for TAU in -2.4..-3.0 step 0.1, recount\n#   how many ladder cells and how many archived scan.jsonl rows flag. Report the smallest\n#   TAU shift producing a first scan false positive, and state plainly that TAU is\n#   panel-fitted and never validated out of panel (margin 0.0763 log10; nearest\n#   non-abliterated neighbours allenai/OLMo-1B-hf -2.6652, rinna -2.614, stablelm -2.515).\n# S6.5 Judge integrity: report Cohen's kappa judge-vs-regex-screen per stage and the\n#   rate-level Pearson r across all stages (archive: r = 0.952, kappa ~ 0 on abliterated\n#   stages). Confirm or correct.\n# S6.6 Cumulative OpenRouter spend, printed after every stage and in the final output.\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# S7. OUTPUTS                                           (target: 20 min)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# results/repro_gate.json          -- every archived value vs recomputed, with deltas\n# results/arm1_framing.json        -- was the archived int4 W05 already dequantized?\n# results/arm1_dequant.jsonl       -- scheme/bits x {W01..W05, W05rel, e_v1, cos, behaviour}\n# results/ladder_with_ci.jsonl     -- archived ladder + Wilson intervals + bootstrap diffs\n# results/roots.json               -- roots A/B/C, direction sweeps, gates, e_v1 profiles\n# results/crossing_table.jsonl     -- THE machine-readable deliverable:\n#     {root, family, intensity, W01, W02, W03, W04, W05, W05q10, W05rel,\n#      refusal_rate, n_harmful, wilson_lo, wilson_hi, refusal_rate_40item,\n#      overrefusal_rate, n_xstest, wikitext_ppl, distinct3, flag_fires, judge_usd}\n# results/threshold_sweep.json     -- TAU -2.4..-3.0 hit counts (ladder + wild scan)\n# results/cost.jsonl               -- one line per judge call, running total\n# results/judge_cache.json         -- so a rerun costs $0\n# method_out.json                  -- verdicts (S2.3, S6.2, S6.3), headline numbers,\n#                                     achieved n per cell, spend, wall clock, and an\n#                                     explicit LIMITATIONS list including every gate\n#                                     delta and any substituted model.\n",

### [26] ASSISTANT · 2026-08-13 23:54:41 UTC

```
Plan written to `./.terminal_claude_agent_struct_out.json`.

**Title:** Does dequantizing bring the scar back? · **Profile:** `gpu`

Key points I grounded against the actual archive (I read the files rather than trusting the direction text):

- **The direction's file names are wrong on disk.** It calls for `wstats.py`; the real modules are `gen_art_experiment_2/lib_metrics.py` and `gen_art_experiment_1/vendored_lib_metrics.py`. The plan makes locating them step one and forbids reimplementing W01–W05 from prose.
- **`root_recipe.json` verified**: `parent_repo` Qwen/Qwen3-1.7B, `l_star` 18, 56 write-matrix keys (o_proj + down_proj × 28 layers), a 2048-float direction `r`. `root.json` gives the exact gate targets — parent W05 `-1.0098422523532755`, root W05 `-4.591675454758807`, W02 exactly 1.0, ppl 26.2518, refusal 0.162 at n=40, and the V_AUROC dissociation row (refusal 0.333, same signature).
- **ARM 1 has a trap I wrote a resolution for.** "Dequantize back to fp16" cannot restore anything if the archived int4 W05 was *already* computed on dequantized tensors — it recovers the rounded values, not the originals. The plan makes the executor resolve that first, and specifies a substantive version either way: a bit-width sweep (8/6/5/4/3) locating where the scar dies, the per-layer energy profile naming which layers lost suppression, `cos(v1_quantized, r)` to separate "null filled in" from "eigenvector rotated", and a new **W05rel** (min-layer energy relative to the 256-random-direction floor) as the actual candidate remedy — with a pre-run sanity check that drops it if it fails to separate parent from root even in fp16.
- **Cost is not the binding constraint.** Archived judge spend was ~$0.0026 per 40 items, so the whole grid (~3 roots × 12 intensities + n=120 re-scores) lands near $0.15 against the $1.50 cap. Response caching makes reruns $0. Wall clock and the gate are the real risks, so the fallback plan gives an explicit drop-order with S3.1 (Wilson intervals on the archived ladder, pure re-analysis, ~15 min) second so a deliverable is guaranteed early.
- **Root B is written so it cannot fail silently**: if the depth-weighted root sits above the threshold unlaundered, the ladder is undefined for it and that is the verdict; and if the only kernels that un-censor turn out to be effectively uniform, the plan requires reporting that as evidence *against* the uniformity story rather than burying it.
```
